## 002067：發現不相交子網 <value>。 · 解決方案

**id**　64239d15e07a75ccab-2

**english**　002067: Disjoint subnetwork was discovered <value>.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 2001-2100

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02051-02075-002067.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02051-02075-002067.html

**text**　查看子網所在的特定層的網絡屬性，以確定其是否支持不相交子網。
如果希望使用不相交子網，則公共設施網絡管理員需要在層的子網定義中啟用對不相交子網的支持。
如果不相交子網不受支持，請在網絡中進行相應編輯以確保子網控制器可遍歷，或者在修改子網控制器窗格中對其中一個子網控制器的子網名稱進行更改。 提示： 查看子網表并按照子網名稱列進行排序。 使用錯誤中提到的子網名稱來檢查設置為子網控制器的圖徵，并確保其處于可遍歷狀態。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02051-02075-002067.html#solution

## ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server 中的空間關系 · ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server 中的空間關系

**id**　642db665c47295286b-1

**english**　Spatial relationships in ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Server 工具箱
- GeoAnalytics 概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html

**text**　連接圖徵 工具允許您根據相對位置將一個圖層中的圖徵連接到另一個圖層中的圖徵。 這稱為空間連接。 與空間連接一起， 連接圖徵 工具也可用于應用 時態連接 和屬性連接。 在定義空間連接時，有多個空間關系可用于選擇一個圖層中的點、線或面圖徵，它們可顯示與另一個圖層中的圖徵的感興趣關系。
例如，要了解受近期洪水影響的房屋數量，您可以使用 Within 空間關系運算符將 Homes 圖層與洪水邊界連接。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html

**text**　空間連接是指根據圖徵的相對空間位置將連接圖層中的圖徵與目標圖層中的圖徵相匹配。
以下圖例適用于本主題中的所有示例：
目標圖徵 - 綠色
連接圖徵 - 紫色
目標圖徵，用于顯示與連接圖徵的所選空間關系，以紅色高亮顯示

## ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server 中的空間關系 · 等于

**id**　642db665c47295286b-15

**english**　Spatial relationships in ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Server 工具箱
- GeoAnalytics 概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html#13C

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html

**text**　如果目標圖徵和連接圖徵的內部完全相同且幾何類型一致，則目標圖徵等于連接圖徵。
在下圖中，當綠色目標圖徵與紫色圖徵相等時，將以紅色輪廓表示目標圖徵：

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html#13C

## ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server 中的空間關系 · 相交

**id**　642db665c47295286b-16

**english**　Spatial relationships in ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Server 工具箱
- GeoAnalytics 概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html#E00

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html

**text**　如果目標圖徵將其任何一部分幾何與連接圖徵共享，則目標圖徵與連接圖徵相交。 如果目標圖徵包含連接圖徵、位于連接圖徵范圍內或者與連接圖徵交叉、接觸或重疊，則目標圖徵為相交圖徵。
在下圖中，當綠色目標圖徵與紫色圖徵相交時，將以紅色輪廓表示目標圖徵：

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html#E00

## ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server 中的空間關系 · 包含

**id**　642db665c47295286b-17

**english**　Spatial relationships in ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Server 工具箱
- GeoAnalytics 概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html#BB0

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html

**text**　如果連接圖徵是目標圖徵的子集（具有相交的內部），則目標圖徵包含連接圖徵。 如果連接圖徵位于邊界上且與內部相交，則目標圖徵包含連接圖徵。 如果連接圖徵僅位于邊界上（而非內部），則目標圖徵不包含連接圖徵。
該運算符與 Within 運算符執行的操作相反。
在下圖中，當綠色目標圖徵包含紫色圖徵時，將以紅色輪廓表示目標圖徵：

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html#BB0

## ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server 中的空間關系 · 位于

**id**　642db665c47295286b-18

**english**　Spatial relationships in ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Server 工具箱
- GeoAnalytics 概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html#EF8

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html

**text**　如果目標圖徵完全位于連接圖徵的內部，則目標圖徵位于連接圖徵范圍內。 如果內部的交集非空且目標圖徵是連接圖徵的子集，則圖徵在范圍內。 如果不存在相交的內部，則連接圖徵不在目標圖徵的范圍內。
該運算符與 Contains 運算符執行的操作相反。
在下圖中，當綠色目標圖徵在紫色圖徵的范圍內時，將以紅色輪廓表示目標圖徵：

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html#EF8

## ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server 中的空間關系 · 交叉

**id**　642db665c47295286b-19

**english**　Spatial relationships in ArcGIS GeoAnalytics Server

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- GeoAnalytics Server 工具箱
- GeoAnalytics 概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html#A1F

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html

**text**　如果折線的交集僅包含點且至少一個交點位于兩條折線內，則兩條折線交叉。 如果折線的連接部分中一部分位于面內，一部分位于面外，則折線與面交叉。 如果折線和面在面的內部共用一條折線（不等于整條折線），則折線與面交叉。 目標圖徵與連接圖徵必須是折線或面。
在下圖中，當綠色目標圖徵與紫色圖徵交叉時，將以紅色輪廓表示目標圖徵：

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/big-data-analytics/spatial-relationships-with-big-data.html#A1F

**text**　從概念上講，這些運算符可通過指定距離緩衝目標圖徵，并返回與應用了緩衝區的連接圖徵相交的所有目標圖徵。
在 ArcGIS Enterprise 10.7 中，GeoAnalytics Server 還支持 Geodesic Near 操作。 此操作與 Planar Near 相同，只是其使用測地線距離，而非平面距離。 如果資料涵蓋較大地理范圍或輸入的座標系為地理座標系，請使用此運算符。
在下圖中，當綠色目標圖徵（具有鄰近距離緩衝區（淺綠色））與紫色連接圖徵鄰近時，將以紅色輪廓表示目標圖徵：

## Export AIXM 5.1 Message (Aviation Tools) · 匯總

**id**　643a6777deb7bb6d1e-1

**english**　Export AIXM 5.1 Message

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料交換工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html

**text**　Exports aeronautical data to an Aeronautical Information Exchange Model (AIXM) 5.1 message.
An AIXM message is a standard data exchange format for aeronautical data. AIXM is a well-established information exchange format in the aviation domain. Using aeronautical data in this format is an important step in the creation of an Aviation Airports or Aviation Charting product. This tool allows you to export data from an Aeronautical Information System (AIS) schema or Esri Airports geodatabase as an AIXM message.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#summary

## Export AIXM 5.1 Message (Aviation Tools) · 用法

**id**　643a6777deb7bb6d1e-2

**english**　Export AIXM 5.1 Message

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料交換工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html

**text**　The Input Aviation Workspace parameter value must be a valid AIS schema or Esri Airports geodatabase workspace.
This tool exports data to an AIXM 5.1 message that is saved as an .xml file.
If you do not select a value for the Input Filter Layers parameter, the tool will run on all of the layers in the geodatabase. Once you apply a filter on one layer, you must apply a filter on all of the layers you want to export.
The export will honor definition queries set on the Input Filter Layers parameter value.
The following AIS entities can be used for the input feature layers: ADHP, ADHPCollocation, Airspace, ADHPSurfaceArea, ADHPSurfaceElement, ADHPSurfacePoint, ADHPSurfaceLine, ADHPThreshold, AirspaceAuth, ATSRoute, DesignatedPoint, EnrouteInformation, Frequency, GeoBorder, Marking, NavaidComponent, NavaidSystem, ObstacleArea, ObstacleStructure, OrgAuth, Service, SurfaceLightSys, and Unit.
Related AIS features will be included in the export message pursuant to mappings in the export configuration files. The export configuration files are 51ExportRelationships.json and 51ExportTemplate.xml and are under C:\Program Files\ArcGIS\Pro\Resources\Aviation.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#usage

## Export AIXM 5.1 Message (Aviation Tools) · 用法

**id**　643a6777deb7bb6d1e-3

**english**　Export AIXM 5.1 Message

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料交換工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html

**text**　The following Esri Airports entities can be used for input feature layers: AirfieldLight, AirportControlPoint, AirportSign, FrequencyArea, MarkingArea, NavaidEquipment, NavaidSite, Obstacle, ObstacleArea, Runway, RunwayEnd, and TaxiwayElement.
Related Esri Airports features will be included in the export message pursuant to mappings in the export configuration files. The following export configuration files are available under C:\Program Files\ArcGIS\Pro\Resources\Aviation: AMDB_AIXM_ExportDomains.json AMDB_AIXM_51ExportRelationships.json AMDB_AIXM_51ExportTemplate.xml
If the Validate Output parameter is checked, you must be connected to the internet for the validation to complete.
If you validate the output AIXM 5.1 message, you can store it in the Output Validation Log parameter value.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#usage

- 標注
- 說明

**values**　
- Export Type
- Specifies the AIXM temporality type the message represents. Baseline—The message contains all features in a given message. Snapshot—The message contains all features at a specific point in time. Permanent Delta—The message contains updates in a given time slice in features as a result of a baseline update. Temporary Delta—The message contains changes for some features in a given time slice representing a temporary event.
- String

## Export AIXM 5.1 Message (Aviation Tools) · 參數

**id**　643a6777deb7bb6d1e-10

**english**　Export AIXM 5.1 Message

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料交換工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html

**parameter**　from_time

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- From Time （可選）
- The starting time that will be applied to the output message for a given feature that is being exported for the field validTime\TimePeriod\beginPosition when the corresponding database value ValidFrom_Date is missing, or to featureLifetime\TimePeriod\beginPosition when the corresponding database value FeatureFrom_Date is missing. This parameter will be used differently depending on the Export Type parameter value as follows: Baseline—The parameter will be honored only when the actual database record does not have the FeatureFrom_Date or ValidFrom_Date attributes populated. Snapshot—The parameter value provided will be exported regardless of the FeatureFrom_Date and ValidFrom_Date attributes in the database. Permanent Delta—The parameter will be honored only when the actual database record does not have the FeatureFrom_Date or ValidFrom_Date attributes populated. Temporary Delta—The parameter will be honored only when the actual database record does not have the FeatureFrom_Date or ValidFrom_Date attributes populated.
- Date

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#parameters

- To Time （可選）
- The ending time that will be applied to the validTime\endPosition and featureLifetime\endPosition fields in the output message types for any missing ValidTo_Date or FeatureTo_Date field values in the features to export. The value will be converted to UTC. If no value is provided, the current system date and time in UTC will be applied to the missing field values in the output message. This parameter is only valid when the Export Type parameter is set to Temporary Delta.
- Date

**parameter**　from_time

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- from_time （可選）
- The starting time that will be applied to the output message for a given feature that is being exported for the field validTime\TimePeriod\beginPosition when the corresponding database value ValidFrom_Date is missing, or to featureLifetime\TimePeriod\beginPosition when the corresponding database value FeatureFrom_Date is missing. This parameter will be used differently depending on the export_type parameter value as follows: BASELINE—The parameter will be honored only when the actual database record does not have the FeatureFrom_Date or ValidFrom_Date attributes populated. SNAPSHOT—The parameter value provided will be exported regardless of the FeatureFrom_Date and ValidFrom_Date attributes in the database. PERM_DELTA—The parameter will be honored only when the actual database record does not have the FeatureFrom_Date or ValidFrom_Date attributes populated. TEMP_DELTA—The parameter will be honored only when the actual database record does not have the FeatureFrom_Date or ValidFrom_Date attributes populated.
- Date

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#parameters

## Export AIXM 5.1 Message (Aviation Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　643a6777deb7bb6d1e-25

**english**　Export AIXM 5.1 Message

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料交換工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html

**text**　# Name: ExportAIXM51Message_example
# Description: Exports aeronautical data to an AIXM 5.1 message.

import arcpy


# Set variables
in_aviation_workspace = "D:\\data\\AIS.gdb"
out_message_file = "C:\\Users\\user1\\Documents\\My AIXM Message.xml"
export_type = "TEMP_DELTA"
last_modified_time = "1/1/2001 12:01:00 AM"
in_filter_layers = None
from_time = "1/1/2000 12:01:00 AM"
to_time = "1/1/2002 12:01:00 AM"
validate_output = "VALIDATE"
out_validation_log = "C:\\Users\\user1\\Documents\\Output Validation Log.txt"

# Run ExportAIXM51Message
arcpy.aviation.ExportAIXM51Message(in_aviation_workspace, out_message_file, export_type, last_modified_time, in_filter_layers,
                                    from_time, to_time, validate_output, out_validation_log)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#parameters

## Export AIXM 5.1 Message (Aviation Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料交換工具集

**english**　Export AIXM 5.1 Message

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html

**id**　643a6777deb7bb6d1e-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Export AIXM 5.1 Message，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input Aviation Workspace [in_aviation_workspace]、Output Message File [out_message_file]、Export Type [export_type]、Last Modified Time （可選） [last_modified_time]、Input Filter Layers （可選） [in_filter_layers]、From Time （可選） [from_time]、To Time （可選） [to_time]、Validate Output （可選） [validate_output]、Output Validation Log （可選） [out_validation_log]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html

**id**　643a6777deb7bb6d1e-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/aviation/export-aixm-5-1-message.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_aviation_workspace", "label_zh": "Input Aviation Workspace", "label_en": "Input Aviation Workspace", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Workspace", "description_en": "The AIS schema or Esri Airports geodatabase workspace."}, {"parameter": "out_message_file", "label_zh": "Output Message File", "label_en": "Output Message File", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "The exported AIXM 5.1 message as an .xml file."}, {"parameter": "export_type", "label_zh": "Export Type", "label_en": "Export Type", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the AIXM temporality type the message represents. Baseline—The message contains all features in a given message. Snapshot—The message contains all features at a specific point in time. Permanent Delta—The message contains updates in a given time slice in features as a result of a baseline update. Temporary Delta—The message contains changes for some features in a given time slice representing a temporary event."}, {"parameter": "last_modified_time", "label_zh": "Last Modified Time （可選）", "label_en": "Last Modified Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Date", "description_en": "The date that will be used to filter the output to only features modified after that date."}, {"parameter": "in_filter_layers", "label_zh": "Input Filter Layers （可選）", "label_en": "Input Filter Layers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer; Table", "description_en": "The layers that will filter output to a smaller spatial subset. The input layers should be AIXM 5.1 feature types. This value should be in the same AIS geodatabase as the Input Aviation Workspace parameter value."}, {"parameter": "from_time", "label_zh": "From Time （可選）", "label_en": "From Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Date", "description_en": "The starting time that will be applied to the output message for a given feature that is being exported for the field validTime\\TimePeriod\\beginPosition when the corresponding database value ValidFrom_Date is missing, or to featureLifetime\\TimePeriod\\beginPosition when the corresponding database value FeatureFrom_Date is missing. This parameter will be used differently depending on the Export Type parameter value as follows: Baseline—The parameter will be honored only when the actual database record does not have the FeatureFrom_Date or ValidFrom_Date attributes populated. Snapshot—The parameter value provided will be exported regardless of the FeatureFrom_Date and ValidFrom_Date attributes in the database. Permanent Delta—The parameter will be honored only when the actual database record does not have the FeatureFrom_Date or ValidFrom_Date attributes populated. Temporary Delta—The parameter will be honored only when the actual database record does not have the FeatureFrom_Date or ValidFrom_Date attributes populated."}, {"parameter": "to_time", "label_zh": "To Time （可選）", "label_en": "To Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Date", "description_en": "The ending time that will be applied to the validTime\\endPosition and featureLifetime\\endPosition fields in the output message types for any missing ValidTo_Date or FeatureTo_Date field values in the features to export. The value will be converted to UTC. If no value is provided, the current system date and time in UTC will be applied to the missing field values in the output message. This parameter is only valid when the Export Type parameter is set to Temporary Delta."}, {"parameter": "validate_output", "label_zh": "Validate Output （可選）", "label_en": "Validate Output (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the exported message will be validated for the XML format. You must be connected to the internet for validation to succeed. Checked—The exported message will be validated for the XML format. Unchecked—The exported message will not be validated for the XML format. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "out_validation_log", "label_zh": "Output Validation Log （可選）", "label_en": "Output Validation Log (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "The output XML validation log file."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.aviation.ExportAIXM51Message(in_aviation_workspace, out_message_file, export_type, {last_modified_time}, {in_filter_layers}, {from_time}, {to_time}, {validate_output}, {out_validation_log})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_aviation_workspace、out_message_file、export_type、last_modified_time、in_filter_layers、from_time、to_time、validate_output、out_validation_log。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**id**　64452b814fea5be786-1

**english**　010208: The PRJ of grid <value> does not match.

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10201-10225-010208.html#description

## 創建移動地理資料庫 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 工作空間工具集

**english**　Create Mobile Geodatabase

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/create-mobile-geodatabase.html

**id**　645673cde60b026ca5-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/create-mobile-geodatabase.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Create Mobile Geodatabase，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。移動地理資料庫位置 [out_folder_path]、移動地理資料庫名稱 [out_name]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/create-mobile-geodatabase.html#parameters

## Apply Masks From Rules (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　645aa7e50aa161bf33-3

**english**　Apply Masks From Rules

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 制圖優化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-masks-from-rules.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-masks-from-rules.html

**text**　The Make Mask From Rules tool must be run before this tool to create the mask feature classes stored in the mask feature dataset. The mask features created by the Make Mask From Rules tool are used to define the extent of the masks applied to the layers in the map.
The color rules are defined in an XML file. A rule specifies that symbolized features of a particular color be masked by a source feature that has a particular color. The following example rule is based on a map specification that dictates that a mask will be created for any location where the outline of a black symbol (color of 0,0,0,100 CMYK) intersects a piece of black annotation. <RuleGroup> <!-- CMYK Feature Masks --> <Mask Size=".1" Units="Points" Type="Box"/> <Source SymbolPart="Text_Only" LayerType="AnnotationLayer"/> <Target SymbolPart="Outline_Only" LayerType="FeatureLayer"/> <TargetExclusions></TargetExclusions> <Rules> <Rule Name="FeatureMasks_Black_CMYK_1pt"> <MaskFeatureClass>FeatureMasks_Black_CMYK_1pt</MaskFeatureClass> <Source Color="0,0,0,100"/> <Targets> <Target Color="0,0,0,100"/> </Targets> </Rule> </Rules> </RuleGroup> 注: Sample XML masking files are delivered in the Defense Mapping and Topographic Mapping product file installs. Use these sample XML files as a template to create custom masking rules.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-masks-from-rules.html#usage

## Apply Masks From Rules (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　645aa7e50aa161bf33-4

**english**　Apply Masks From Rules

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 制圖優化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-masks-from-rules.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-masks-from-rules.html

**text**　The XML rule file is used to determine the symbol layers (such as fill, outline, and so on) that require masking based on the color of the symbol layer.
Any masks currently applied to the layers in the map will be removed when this tool is run. The resulting map will only have masks applied to layers that meet the results defined in the mask XML.
Set the coordinate system of the map to match the projection of the final printable map or other output product before applying masks. The coordinate system of the map should also match the coordinate system of the mask feature dataset. 注: If the mask feature dataset is not projected to the proper coordinate system, the masks will not align with the features in the final printable map. Set the proper coordinate system on the masks feature dataset before running the Make Masks From Rules tool to avoid mask projection issues.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-masks-from-rules.html#usage

**text**　ApplyMasksFromRules example (stand-alone script)
The following code sample takes masks generated by the MakeMasksFromRules tool and applies them to the features in the specified MAPX file. ApplyMasksFromRules will set symbol level masking on the layers.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

## Apply Masks From Rules (Topographic Production Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　645aa7e50aa161bf33-16

**english**　Apply Masks From Rules

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 制圖優化工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-masks-from-rules.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-masks-from-rules.html

**text**　# Name: ApplyMasksFromRules_sample.py
# Description: This script takes masks that were generated by the Make Masks from Rules tool and applies them to the
# features in the specified mapx file. This tool will set symbol level masking on the layers.

# Import System Modules
import arcpy

# Setting the environment
arcpy.env.overwriteOutput = True

# Setting Local Variables
in_map = r'C:\Data\NewZealandHistoricBuildings.mapx'
rule_file = r'C:\Data\NewZealandHistoricBuildingAnno_Masking_Rules.xml'
in_feature_dataset = r'C:\Data\Label_your_map.gdb\Masks'

# Calling the Apply Masks From Rules to apply masks created in the Make Masks From Rules tool
arcpy.topographic.ApplyMasksFromRules(in_map, rule_file, in_feature_dataset)

# Getting all messages, warnings, and errors from the tool run and printing the results back to the user
messages = arcpy.GetMessages(0)
warnings = arcpy.GetMessages(1)
errors = arcpy.GetMessages(2)
arcpy.AddMessage('Tool Messages: {}\nTool Warnings: {}\nTool Errors{}\n'.format(messages, warnings, errors))

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-masks-from-rules.html#derived-output

## Apply Masks From Rules (Topographic Production Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱

**id**　645aa7e50aa161bf33-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-masks-from-rules.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Apply Masks From Rules，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input Map [in_map]、Rule File [rule_file]、Mask Feature Dataset [in_feature_dataset]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**parameter**　filter_barrier

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 過濾器障礙
- 指定追蹤位置在用作障礙時是否表現為過濾障礙。 了解有關障礙的詳細信息 選中—障礙的行為將與 過濾器障礙 相同。 這對于基于子網的追蹤很有用，在這種追蹤中，障礙允許首先對子網進行評估，然后應用于網絡圖徵的第二次遍歷。 未選中—障礙的行為將與 可遍歷性障礙 相同，可定義子網范圍并在第一次通過時進行評估。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/add-trace-locations.html#parameters

## 添加追蹤位置 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　64631cf7baa3a99d12-15

**english**　Add Trace Locations

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/add-trace-locations.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/add-trace-locations.html

**parameter**　trace_locations

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- trace_locations [[Layer Name, Global ID, Terminal ID, Percent Along],...] （可選）
- 將添加到輸出圖徵類的追蹤位置。 如果在活動地圖中未使用 load_selected_features 參數，則可以使用此參數，通過在值表中提供所需值來指定要作為追蹤位置添加的公共設施網絡圖徵。 值表列： Layer Name—The layer that contains the locations to add. Global ID—The Global ID of the feature for the location to add. Terminal—The terminal ID of the feature for the location to add. Percent Along—The percent along value of the feature. For lines and edge objects, the default value is 0.5.
- Value Table

## 添加追蹤位置 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　64631cf7baa3a99d12-16

**english**　Add Trace Locations

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/add-trace-locations.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/add-trace-locations.html

**parameter**　filter_barrier

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- filter_barrier
- 指定追蹤位置在用作障礙時是否表現為過濾障礙。 了解有關障礙的詳細信息 FILTER_BARRIER—障礙的行為將與 過濾器障礙 相同。 這對于基于子網的追蹤很有用，在這種追蹤中，障礙允許首先對子網進行評估，然后應用于網絡圖徵的第二次遍歷。 TRAVERSABILITY_BARRIER—障礙的行為將與 可遍歷性障礙 相同，可定義子網范圍并在第一次通過時進行評估。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/add-trace-locations.html#parameters

## 添加追蹤位置 (公共設施網絡工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　64631cf7baa3a99d12-20

**english**　Add Trace Locations

arcpy.env.outputZFlag = "Enabled"

# AddTraceLocations
arcpy.un.AddTraceLocations(r"http://landbase.mydomain.com/server/rest/services/NapervilleElectric/FeatureServer/9",

                           "DO_NOT_LOAD_SELECTED_FEATURES",

                           "'Circuit Breaker' {DDB0765D-860A-4054-908D-9360E1A32F74} '(3/Load)' #;'ElecDist Line' {67C0534B-A80D-4E5F-8926-5FB5F887A5F2} # 0.25",
                           "TRAVERSABILITY_BARRIER")

## 添加追蹤位置 (公共設施網絡工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**english**　Add Trace Locations

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/add-trace-locations.html

**id**　64631cf7baa3a99d12-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/add-trace-locations.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Add Trace Locations，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入公共設施網絡 [in_utility_network]、輸出圖徵類 [out_feature_class]、加載所選圖徵 （可選） [load_selected_features]、清除追蹤位置 （可選） [clear_trace_locations]、追蹤位置 （可選） [trace_locations]、過濾器障礙 [filter_barrier]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/add-trace-locations.html#parameters

## 添加追蹤位置 (公共設施網絡工具) · 英文版工具簽章與參數順序

**english**　Add Trace Locations

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/add-trace-locations.html

**id**　64631cf7baa3a99d12-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/add-trace-locations.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/utility-networks/add-trace-locations.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_utility_network", "label_zh": "輸入公共設施網絡", "label_en": "Input Utility Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Utility Network; Utility Network Layer", "description_en": "The input utility network where the trace locations will be added. Important: When the utility network specified for the Input Utility Network parameter contains a telecom domain network, the output trace locations include three additional fields: FIRSTUNIT, LASTUNIT, and COLORSCHEME. These fields must be manually modified in the output feature class to specify the unit identifiers in the grouped objects to which the trace location applies."}, {"parameter": "out_feature_class", "label_zh": "輸出圖徵類", "label_en": "Output Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output feature class containing the trace locations. If you provide a new feature class name, a new output feature class will be created. You can provide the name of an existing feature class and choose to append to any existing trace locations in the feature class, or overwrite these using the Clear Trace Locations parameter. Note: When working with utility network version 7 or later, the output feature class can be created in a mobile, file, or enterprise geodatabase"}, {"parameter": "load_selected_features", "label_zh": "加載所選圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Load Selected Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether selected features in the active map will be loaded as trace locations. Checked—Trace locations will be loaded based on the selection in the map. Unchecked—Trace locations will not be loaded based on the selection in the map. This is the default. However, trace locations can be specified using the Trace Locations parameter."}, {"parameter": "clear_trace_locations", "label_zh": "清除追蹤位置 （可選）", "label_en": "Clear Trace Locations (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether existing trace locations will be cleared from the output feature class. Checked—Existing trace locations will be cleared. Unchecked—Existing trace locations will not be cleared; they will be kept and appended to. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "trace_locations", "label_zh": "追蹤位置 （可選）", "label_en": "Trace Locations (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The trace locations that will be added to the output feature class. If you are not using the Load Selected Features parameter in an active map, you can use this parameter to specify the utility network features to add as trace locations by providing the required values in the value table. Value table columns: Layer Name—The layer that contains the locations to add. Global ID—The Global ID of the feature for the location to add. Terminal—The terminal ID of the feature for the location to add. Percent Along—The percent along value of the feature. For lines and edge objects, the default value is 0.5."}, {"parameter": "filter_barrier", "label_zh": "過濾器障礙", "label_en": "Filter Barrier (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the trace locations will behave as filter barriers when used as barriers. Learn more about barriers Checked—The barriers will behave as filter barriers. This is useful for subnetwork-based traces in which the barrier allows the subnetwork to be evaluated first and is then applied on a second traversal of the network features. Unchecked—The barriers will behave as traversability barriers, which define the extent of subnetworks, and will be evaluated on the first pass. This is the default."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.un.AddTraceLocations(in_utility_network, out_feature_class, {load_selected_features}, {clear_trace_locations}, {trace_locations}, {filter_barrier})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_utility_network、out_feature_class、load_selected_features、clear_trace_locations、trace_locations、filter_barrier。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**id**　64879392f6d202a506-1

## 了解水系 · 了解水系

**id**　64c1249a12123e45ab-1

**english**　Understanding drainage systems

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 水文分析工具集
- 水文分析工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/understanding-drainage-systems.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/understanding-drainage-systems.html

**text**　需具備 Spatial Analyst 許可方可使用。
水落在其上的區域以及水流經并到達出水口的網絡稱為排水系統。 通過排水系統的水流只是通常所說的水文循環的一個子集，水文循環還包括降水、蒸散和地下水流。 水文工具側重于地表水的運動。
流域盆地是將水和其他物質排放到共同出水口的區域。 流域盆地的其他常用術語包括分水嶺、盆地、集水區或匯流區。 該區域通常定義為流向給定出水口或傾瀉點的全部區域。
傾瀉點是水流出一個區域的點。 這通常是沿流域盆地邊界的最低點。
兩個盆地之間的邊界稱為分水嶺或集水區邊界。
可以將流向出水口的水流經的網絡視為樹，樹的底部即為出水口。 樹的分支是河道。 兩條河道的交匯點稱為節點或交匯點。 連接兩個連續交匯點或一個交匯點和出水口的河道部分稱為河流鏈。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/understanding-drainage-systems.html
